# GuardOath engineering coordination — Atlas authority

Updated 2026-11-09. Greg explicitly authorized replacing the empty coordination file and resuming implementation.

## Release stabilization reservation lifted — Greg directive, 2026-11-19

Greg explicitly removed the November 13 release-stabilization reservation and authorized deployment of all eligible completed changes. The reservation no longer blocks main integration or unrelated substantive commits. Coordinate writers and avoid racing commits; a new source SHA must receive its own exact-SHA release checks before deployment. Keep all customer safeguards, fail-closed enforcement, the 500-ms PII deadline, rollback, source parity, cost controls, and the 15 live acceptance checks. Open issue #30 is a disclosed risk, not an automatic issue-state deployment veto; do not claim it resolved. No Director gate. This instruction supersedes earlier freeze references in issues, historical log entries, and archived coordination.

## Governing decisions
- Greg's 2026-11-13 deployment directive: deploy all committed improvements; an open Issue #30 alone must not veto rollout after exact-SHA CI acceptance. Report the unresolved latency risk without closing or declaring it fixed. Keep the 500-ms deadline, privacy fail-closed enforcement, rollback, source parity and 15 live checks.
- Atlas coordinates engineering and owns Field Comparisons issues #28 and #31, including tenant/API/dashboard/tests.
- The Director is removed from active work. Do not request Director acknowledgements or assign Director tasks.
- Work directly on main, preserve concurrent changes, avoid forced updates, and inspect current ownership and CI before edits.
- New validation features are not complete until tenant configuration, API, dashboard, documentation and browser/regression coverage are integrated.
- Registration supports native Cognito email/password; Google federation is optional. Registration does not grant a paid entitlement.
- Stripe subscription activation must come only from trusted provider reconciliation, never a browser checkout redirect.
- Attachment inspection failures must BLOCK even in observe mode; automatic attachment checks require actual bytes unless explicitly disabled.
- Production deployment is authorized, subject to CI, cost controls, rollback and the 15 verification checks.
- Greg's November 23 product discussion and direction to preserve/implement its additions are recorded in [the product roadmap](product-roadmap.md). Its 47 targets and priority order remain durable work items; finish #39–#42 and verified production acceptance first, then update roadmap evidence as work is implemented. Proposed packaging is not a launched-product claim or spending authorization.
- Greg requested meaningful progress updates and durable new-chat continuity. Maintain [the owner admin handoff](admin-console-handoff.md) alongside substantive implementation; it records actual verification, open work and blockers and does not replace fresh main/CI/ownership checks.

## Production release for saved request cap restoration

Owners can use **Tenants → Review cap restoration** to restore an eligible
tenant's monthly allowance to its verified plan limit. The release enabled the
control. No customer allowance was changed during deployment.

Source `9881ddb7f7ab024303f16aa2dceace19e0d03175` passed all 25 required CI jobs.
AWS reported success at `2026-11-27T02:41:18.000+0000`. The deployment verified
the backend and 13 of 13 dashboard assets.
The deployed archive digest is `S+Au9DjT+RcZb94ITHojgfKWQGnalGGUOiXfhgbl9PE=`. The deployment
verified the actual rollback archive for source `9f44394f5d5e278e9fe879b9ed0c903c033632ce`
with digest `sc1Mz7sC+jcAKUir6IsYrbx8iwpmHz6ACzAKuWHTJZQ=` against all six writer contracts.
The completed Lambda revision was `e73eb229-2949-43ba-acd0-2b82127ceb81`.
The 15-area live acceptance and issue #30 remain open.

Earlier activation candidate `acc5e3c` failed billing and rollback tests that
expected five active contracts. Candidate `924ab2e` passed those suites and
exposed two mounted financial tests that expected restoration to be disabled.
The deployed version corrected both assertions.

Next in CORE-04/#41 are credits, configured plan changes and durable processing
of subscription and risk events. [Release tests](https://github.com/CollectiverseStudio/guardoath/actions/runs/45926029744)

## 2026-11-10 Atlas — revisioned Field Comparisons Save isolation on actual customer UI

- Fresh main governance and AGENTS.md read before changes, then current commits/open #28/#31/#30/#32/#33/#36/#37 comments and individual GitHub Actions jobs. Atlas retains all FC #28/#31 source/UI/tenant/release ownership; no separate Codex invocation or conflicting active FC author inferred. Old main `5e5c6bad` had [13 Tests 42896461099](https://github.com/CollectiverseStudio/guardoath/actions/runs/42896461099): **12/13 individual jobs successful, dashboard still running and later superseded/cancelled**, with strict FC [42896461062](https://github.com/CollectiverseStudio/guardoath/actions/runs/42896461062), signed reference [42896461120](https://github.com/CollectiverseStudio/guardoath/actions/runs/42896461120) and CodeQL [42896460223](https://github.com/CollectiverseStudio/guardoath/actions/runs/42896460223) success. No claim of full prior browser acceptance.
- **Concrete source defect:** `saveTenant` in `guardoath/static/dashboard.js` used mutable global API key/tenant after awaiting a revisioned tenant write and its JSON body. A delayed demo/customer Save could reload the *replacement* tenant and display the *previous* tenant's Save success; changing an unsaved Field Comparisons draft during the pending response could also be erased by a stale reload. This is a customer usability and tenant-context isolation problem, not a new Engine/rule bypass.
- **Implemented on non-forced main:** [738ea7f6](https://github.com/CollectiverseStudio/guardoath/commit/738ea7f6834d64311f3747ea87e85f861d776130), [680f5819](https://github.com/CollectiverseStudio/guardoath/commit/680f5819690c58ff1ea312847dee37dcf0db6817), [96b2306b](https://github.com/CollectiverseStudio/guardoath/commit/96b2306bcbd44a879118c1b0b399baf2dbc5ba9f) pin the exact originating key, tenant, revision, Cognito session, and submitted UI draft before the write. On stale completion, return false without reloading, unlocking or relabeling a replacement tenant. A concurrent second draft stays visible with an explicit warning to reconnect and compare the server's authoritative revision. Successful current-owner Save and 409 reload still run through the original shared tenant Engine and controls; no unsafe client assertion that the server canceled the old write.
- **Real browser/API regressions added:** [af462a24](https://github.com/CollectiverseStudio/guardoath/commit/af462a24d5000d73f03c2210b5face59cf7ed554) `tests/browser/field_comparison_save_session_races.spec.js` uses the actual Save checks button and upstream revisioned tenant POST (response held after server processes it), then either connects a different real demo tenant or edits the same tenant again. It awaits the true source promise, checks stale return false, preserved new tenant/draft/tenant-specific status, actual persisted original write and restores demo2 afterwards. No mocked validation Engine or spoofed successful save. [Guide 37b7f7cb](https://github.com/CollectiverseStudio/guardoath/commit/37b7f7cb39320d4e36f58d04ce1d9f5198c396d4) describes reconnect/compare behavior.
- **Actually executed before receipt:** full `dashboard.js` and new browser spec both parsed in V8; extracted the exact current-source `saveTenant` and executed **4/4 synthetic positive/negative async assertions** (legitimate save reload, switched tenant suppressed, second draft preserved/warned, revoked owner session suppressed). These do **not** replace actual Playwright. Exact [strict FC 42905848332](https://github.com/CollectiverseStudio/guardoath/actions/runs/42905848332) **success** at browser test SHA `af462a24` (source unchanged after); latest docs SHA `37b7f7cb39` is tracked separately. Existing example/export confirmation reset retained. Does NOT mutate pending rules, saved tenant revision, check action, private reference, or Engine on a text edit.
- **Integrated customer tests and documentation:** New `tests/browser/fc_bulk_confirmation_reset.spec.js` exercises actual authenticated demo2/tenant/API/dashboard four-family examples, confirms revised JSON cannot Stage without fresh checkbox, preserves initial revision/draft, then stage each independently; only deliberate revisioned Save persists two rules per family. ## 2026-11-13 Atlas — reject PDF headers at CSV/TSV cell starts, authenticated attachment integration; exact SHA 13 Tests + 3 CodeQL GREEN (#32)

- FIRST read current main coordination authority / Greg's 2026-11-07 Atlas-exclusive Field Comparisons #28/#31 override, then AGENTS.md, latest commits/open issues, most recent #28/#31/#30/#32/#33 comments, and actual individual exact-SHA GitHub Actions jobs. FC unchanged: prior last-FC-source [run 43397313073](https://github.com/CollectiverseStudio/guardoath/actions/runs/43397313073) passed 2/2 strict+customer Chrome jobs (feasible/unsatisfiable witnesses, original 30/51/100/250/500 scale, 14 tenant APIs, 147 reference tests, 74/74 real Chrome) at `16f5d7fa`; predecessor source must not be confused with newest SHA.
- **Actual security gap and runtime source:** [f88e871c](https://github.com/CollectiverseStudio/guardoath/commit/f88e871cbe59f4b65eb486a3974a169db9402c4d) with corrected Python byte literals [c1d470a7](https://github.com/CollectiverseStudio/guardoath/commit/c1d470a7036f4739fa33f20eabe7ddbf04fd76df) updates `guardoath/attachment_probe.py`. Former conservative probe rejected PDF headers only at line starts within the first 1,024 bytes. A two-column CSV-like file `column,value\nrow,%PDF-1.7\nrow,1` could be reported recognized although its second cell begins with a PDF header recognizable by a PDF reader. New bounded check rejects early %PDF- at line starts **or CSV/TSV cell starts**, accepting optional leading whitespace, quotes and BOM, returning existing `pdf_parser_unavailable` unsupported/incomplete/Block. Existing benign prose containing `ordinary %PDF-1.7 reference` still recognizes as CSV. This is intentionally conservative, NOT full PDF or polyglot inspection. No tenant/Engine/check policy, storage, customer controls, cost or deadline change.

## 2026-11-19 Atlas — admin MFA signed-proof correction and immutable login freshness
- AWS Cognito current documentation confirms that `UserPoolTier=LITE` with `ManagedLoginVersion=1` provides classic hosted UI, which does not furnish dependable signed per-login AMR MFA evidence, while Essentials/Plus managed login is a tier change; no unauthorized paid tier upgrade is permitted.
- Found a security defect in owner verifier: signed `amr=["otp"]` or `["software_token"]` previously satisfied a loose *any-of* MFA marker, even though a single method is not proof of multiple factors, and refreshed sessions could preserve old AMR without a freshness check. Strengthened `guardoath/admin_cognito.py` to require signed explicit `mfa` marker + signed integer `auth_time` within 15 minutes (30-second clock-skew window). Current Cognito provider active-user + independent owner group checks retained. Regression updates in `tests/test_admin_cognito.py` and `tests/test_admin_integration.py` cover one-factor rejection, missing/stale/future timestamps, refresh replay and valid current MFA. `docs/admin-console-operator.md` records the tier blocker. No Global Engine or tenant logic, AWS/Stripe write, payment, timeout relaxation or test skip. Exact new-SHA full CI required after commit.

## 2026-11-23 — rollback is conditional on the deployment's own revision receipt
- Completed exact catalog source acceptance at `276a187191cc326d1b1106849b11345df0d7a519`: all eight triggered workflows / 26 individually inspected jobs succeeded (seven required / 25 plus Red-team). Tests 45310982995 (13), CodeQL 45310983347 (3), Field Comparisons 45310982947 (2), Trusted Reference 45310982959 (1), PII 45310983071 (3), Stripe 45310982956 (2), Deploy Script 45310983080 (1), Red-team 45310982950 (1). Actual dashboard job 138166353168 passed 4 baseline owner, 7 retained writer owner, 19 guarded-invocation and 462 default browser tests. No failed, missing, cancelled or skipped job. This is exact-source offline CI, not production acceptance.
- Fixed a concrete pre-existing rollback authority bug in `deploy/aws/Deploy-Verified.ps1`: `$Uploaded` is set before an update whose response may be lost or rejected, but the former catch read the latest Lambda revision and used it to restore. If another deployment had won, that could overwrite its newer image. The script now hashes the candidate, validates the actual update response and retains only its returned revision. Candidate acceptance independently checks hash/revision/success state after waiting.
- New actual `Restore-VerifiedLambdaBackup` requires current candidate hash plus that retained owned revision and a terminal update before any restore. Lost/malformed original responses, different code or revision (including same-code configuration changes), and InProgress refuse automatic restoration. The write uses the retained original revision condition, never a newly observed revision, and never retries a race or lost restore response. Existing backup digest is rechecked before writing; restoration then requires verified backup bytes, returned new revision and Successful status. Already-present backup code is observation-only. Digest/revision comparisons are case-sensitive, raw AWS configuration is not exposed, and the original deployment failure remains a failure even if recovery succeeds.
- Added `tests/powershell/rollback_ownership.ps1` importing actual production helpers, with real ZIP/hash verification and only scripted AWS responses. Covers owned terminal success/failure, original backup already present, third-party code, same code/new revision, unknown original outcome, in-progress candidate/backup, racing writes, lost restore response, wrong restore hash/revision/final state, case differences, malformed/private configuration and tampered backup. Existing compatibility tests now require specific refusal reasons and still verify the actual restore path. Integrated the new suite into Deploy Script Acceptance without removing a gate.
- Actual local verification: **all six PowerShell steps from the real workflow passed**, using official PowerShell 7.4.6 with its release archive checksum verified (`6f6015203c47806c5cc444c19d8ed019695e610fbd948154264bf9ca8e157561`); no cloud boundary was contacted. Python rollback/archive/build/template suites **65 passed**. Whitespace and independent read-only source/test reviews passed. New exact-SHA full CI remains required. Updated operator/handoff documentation with verified catalog evidence and new rollback semantics. Root sole writer, no AWS deployment/provider mutation/spend, no live rollback/financial/source-parity/15-check claim. #39–#42 and the disclosed #30 risk remain open.
